# Skills · C · part 144

858 entries, coalesce-review-risk to cocktail-syrup-conversion. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **coalesce-review-risk**: Use when reviewing Coalesce pipeline changes for correctness, impact, and risk before approval — READ-ONLY analysis of diffs, coa validate/dry-run evidence, and downstream blast radius. Never edits files or runs warehouse-mutating commands. ([Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills](https://github.com/Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/coalesce-review-risk))
- **coalesce-sql-transformation**: Use when editing the SQL inside existing Coalesce V2 node files (.sql) — changing column expressions, joins, filters, CTEs, inline column annotations, or ref() macros. Not for creating/deleting node files (use coalesce-pipeline-structure). ([Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills](https://github.com/Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/coalesce-sql-transformation))
- **coalesce-transform-mcp-install**: Use when installing, connecting, or troubleshooting the hosted Coalesce Transform MCP server in an MCP client (Claude Code, Claude Desktop, Cursor, VS Code, Cortex Code) — endpoint URL, access token handling, registration, and verification. Not for coa CLI auth (use coalesce-cloud-api). ([Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills](https://github.com/Coalesce-Software-Inc/coalesce-platform-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/coalesce-transform-mcp-install))
- **coalescent-genealogy-plotting**: Use when teaching or demonstrating genetic drift and coalescence (Kingman, 1982): you need to simulate allele lineages in a Wright–Fisher population and render the resulting coalescent genealogy as a plot or exported flat graphic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/coalescent-genealogy-plotting))

- **coalface**: Fan-out discipline for swarm work. When a task decomposes into many units (a bulk refactor, a repo-wide sweep, a corpus batch), CoalFace runs it as a disciplined factory: mandatory SCOUT surveys the worksite, deterministic PARTITION merges overlapping/tiny spots, workers return anchor-edit orders as TEXT (propose-not-execute), QC checks scope+spec at collection, main = SINGLE WRITER (pre-swarm snapshot + domain gate), RECEIPT shows tokens-vs-solo. Wallet: ~solo DOLLAR cost only on cheaper worker tiers (raw tokens run HIGHER — fan-out ×N the per-sub baseline), not raw tokens. Modes: coalfaceMo… ([TheColliery/CoalFace](https://github.com/TheColliery/CoalFace/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/coalface) · ★8 · 2 copies)

- **cockroachdb-sum-widens-to-decimal**: Use when writing aggregate queries against CockroachDB integer columns, especially through an ORM. SUM over an INT returns DECIMAL, so the familiar coalesce(sum(x), 0) pairs a decimal with an integer literal and CockroachDB rejects the statement outright with "incompatible COALESCE expressions" — a runtime 500 that neither typechecking nor a unit test with a mocked database will catch. Covers the cast that fixes it and the class of type-widening surprises it belongs to. ([halmahera-dev/knowledge-compiler](https://github.com/halmahera-dev/knowledge-compiler/tree/HEAD/skills/application-development/cockroachdb-sum-widens-to-decimal))
